Query handles that lifecycle directly instead of making you recreate it with reducers, effects, and synchronized stores.</p></div><div class="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2"><div class="rounded-lg border 30_000<br/>gcTime: 300_000</p></div></div><div><p class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-black uppercase text-red-700 dark:text-red-300"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="14" class="th-code th-code--tsx" data-language="tsx"><code><span class="th-token th-keyword">import</span> { useQuery } <span class="th-token th-keyword">from</span> <span class="th-token <span class="th-token th-keyword">function</span> <span class="th-token th-function">Todos</span>() {
  <span class="th-token th-keyword">const</span> { data, isPending, error } = <span class="th-token th-function">useQuery</span>({
    queryKey: [<span class="th-token th-string">&#39;todos&#39;</span>],
    queryFn: () =&gt; <span class="th-token th-function">fetch</span>(<span class="th-token th-string">&#39;/api/todos&#39;</span>).<span class="th-token th-property">then</span>(r =&gt; r.<span class="th-token th-property">json</span>()),
  })

  <span class="th-token th-keyword">if</span> (isPending) <span class="th-token th-keyword">return</span> &lt;<span class="th-token th-tag">span</span>&gt;<span class="th-token th-type">Loading</span>...&lt;/<span class="th-token th-tag">span</span>&gt;
  <span class="th-token th-keyword">if</span> (error) <span class="th-token th-keyword">return</span> &lt;<span class="th-token th-tag">span</span>&gt;<span class="th-token th-type">Oops</span>!&lt;/<span class="th-token th-tag">span</span>&gt;

  <span class="th-token th-keyword">return</span> &lt;<span class="th-token th-tag">ul</span>&gt;{data.<span class="th-token th-property">map</span>(t =&gt; &lt;<span class="th-token th-tag">li</span> <span class="th-token th-attr">key</span>={t.<span class="th-token th-property">id</span>}&gt;{t.<span class="th-token th-property">title</span>}&lt;/<span class="th-token th-tag">li</span>&gt;)}&lt;/<span class="th-token th-tag">ul</span>&gt;
}

<span class="th-token th-keyword">export</span> <span class="th-token th-keyword">default</span> <span class="th-token th-type">Todos</span></code></pre></div></div></div><div data-tab="preact" class="max-w-none flex-col gap-2 text-base hidden"><div class="codeblock w-full max-w-full relative not-prose border-gray-500/20 rounded-md overflow-hidden [&amp;_pre]:rounded-none mt-0 border-0"><div class=""><pre class="th-code th-code--tsx" data-language="tsx"><code><span class="th-token th-keyword">import</span> { useQuery } <span class="th-token th-keyword">from</span> <span class="th-token th-string">&#39;@tanstack/preact-query&#39;</span>

<span class="th-token th-keyword">function</span> <span class="th-token th-function">Todos</span>() {
  <span class="th-token th-keyword">const</span> { data, isPending, error } = <span class="th-token th-function">useQuery</span>({
    queryKey: [<span class="th-token th-string">&#39;todos&#39;</span>],
    queryFn: () =&gt; <span class="th-token th-function">fetch</span>(<span class="th-token th-string">&#39;/api/todos&#39;</span>).<span class="th-token th-property">then</span>(r =&gt; r.<span class="th-token th-property">json</span>()),

  <span class="th-token th-keyword">if</span> (error) <span class="th-token th-keyword">return</span> &lt;<span class="th-token th-tag">span</span>&gt;<span class="th-token th-type">Oops</span>!&lt;/<span class="th-token th-tag">span</span>&gt;

<span class="th-token th-keyword">export</span> <span class="th-token th-keyword">default</span> <span class="th-token th-type">Todos</span></code></pre></div></div></div><div data-tab="solid" class="max-w-none flex-col gap-2 text-base hidden"><div class="codeblock w-full max-w-full relative not-prose border-gray-500/20 rounded-md overflow-hidden [&amp;_pre]:rounded-none mt-0 border-0"><div class=""><pre class="th-code th-code--tsx" data-language="tsx"><code><span class="th-token th-keyword">import</span> { createQuery } <span class="th-token th-keyword">from</span> <span class="th-token th-string">&#39;@tanstack/solid-query&#39;</span>

<span class="th-token th-keyword">function</span> <span class="th-token th-function">Todos</span>() {
  <span class="th-token th-keyword">const</span> todos = <span class="th-token th-function">createQuery</span>(() =&gt; ({
    queryKey: [<span class="th-token th-string">&#39;todos&#39;</span>],
    queryFn: () =&gt; <span class="th-token th-function">fetch</span>(<span class="th-token th-string">&#39;/api/todos&#39;</span>).<span class="th-token th-property">then</span>(r =&gt; r.<span class="th-token th-property">json</span>()),
  }))
